const BOOK=psychdeskBookData, CHAPTERS=BOOK.chapters, PARTS=BOOK.studyParts;
const KEY=BOOK.storageKey;
const $=(q,r=document)=>r.querySelector(q), $$=(q,r=document)=>[...r.querySelectorAll(q)];
const escapeHTML=(s)=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const icon=(id)=>`<svg aria-hidden="true"><use href="#ic-${id}"></use></svg>`;
const defaults={version:1,done:[],bookmarks:[],notes:{},highlights:[],resume:{partId:PARTS[0].id,percent:0},positions:{},settings:{theme:'sepia',font:'Georgia',size:19,leading:1.78,width:720},focus:false,sidebarCollapsed:false};
function initState(){let v={};try{v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY)||'{}')||{}}catch(_e){};return {...defaults,...v,done:Array.isArray(v.done)?v.done:[],bookmarks:Array.isArray(v.bookmarks)?v.bookmarks:[],notes:v.notes&&typeof v.notes==='object'?v.notes:{},highlights:Array.isArray(v.highlights)?v.highlights:[],positions:v.positions&&typeof v.positions==='object'?v.positions:{},resume:v.resume?.partId?v.resume:defaults.resume,settings:{...defaults.settings,...(v.settings||{})},sidebarCollapsed:typeof v.sidebarCollapsed==='boolean'?v.sidebarCollapsed:defaults.sidebarCollapsed};}
let state=initState(), currentPart=null,currentPdfPage=3+18,pdfUrl=null,pdfFileName='',drawerType=null,drawerFocusRestore=null,dialogFocusRestore=null,toastTimeout=null,selectionDraft=null,routeBusy=false;
function save(){try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(state))}catch(e){toast('Browser storage is unavailable; progress may not persist.')}}
function toast(msg){const el=$('#toast');el.textContent=msg;el.hidden=false;clearTimeout(toastTimeout);toastTimeout=setTimeout(()=>el.hidden=true,3100)}
function applySettings(){const s=state.settings;document.body.dataset.theme=['sepia','white','dark'].includes(s.theme)?s.theme:'sepia';document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--size',`${Math.max(15,Math.min(28,+s.size||19))}px`);document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--leading',String(Math.max(1.4,Math.min(2.4,+s.leading||1.78))));document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--measure',`${Math.max(520,Math.min(920,+s.width||720))}px`);document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--body-font',s.font==='system'?"system-ui,-apple-system,'Segoe UI',sans-serif":s.font==='Palatino'?"'Palatino Linotype',Palatino,Georgia,serif":"Georgia,'Times New Roman',serif");document.body.classList.toggle('focus',!!state.focus);}
function applySidebarState(){document.body.classList.toggle('sidebar-collapsed',!!state.sidebarCollapsed);const button=$('#topbar [data-open="contents"]');if(!button)return;const desktop=document.body.dataset.screen==='reader'&&window.matchMedia('(min-width:1040px)').matches&&!state.focus;if(desktop){button.setAttribute('aria-controls','studySidebar');button.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(!state.sidebarCollapsed));button.setAttribute('aria-label',state.sidebarCollapsed?'Show chapter contents':'Hide chapter contents');button.title=state.sidebarCollapsed?'Show contents':'Hide contents';}else{button.removeAttribute('aria-controls');button.removeAttribute('aria-expanded');button.setAttribute('aria-label','Open contents and topic index');button.title='Contents';}}
function fmtPage(a,b){return a===b?`p. ${a}`:`pp. ${a}–${b}`}
function isDone(id){return state.done.includes(id)}
function chapterProgress(c){const ids=c.modules.map(m=>m.readingPartId);const read=ids.filter(isDone).length;return {read,total:ids.length,pct:ids.length?Math.round(read*100/ids.length):0}}
function setRoute(route){if(location.hash.slice(1)===route){renderRoute();return}location.hash=route}
function getHash(){return decodeURIComponent(location.hash.slice(1))}
function readPart(id,restore=false){let found=PARTS.find(p=>p.id===id)||PARTS[0];if(found.id===state.resume.partId&&restore){}else{state.resume={partId:found.id,percent:0};save()}setRoute('read='+encodeURIComponent(found.id));}
function pdfJump(p){currentPdfPage=Number(p)||21;setRoute('pdf='+currentPdfPage)}
function navbar(){const isreader=!!currentPart;const ispdf=document.body.dataset.screen==='pdf';const bookmarksActive=isreader&&state.bookmarks.includes(currentPart.id);return `<div class="top-start"><button type="button" class="brand" id="homeBtn" title="Go to bookshelf"> <span class="brand-glyph" aria-hidden="true">P</span><span class="brand-text">PsychDesk</span></button>${isreader||ispdf?`<button class="iconbtn" type="button" id="libraryBack" aria-label="Back to bookshelf" title="Bookshelf">${icon('back')}</button>`:''}</div><div class="top-caption"><strong>Biological Psychology</strong><small>${isreader?`Module ${PARTS.findIndex(p=>p.id===currentPart.id)+1} of ${PARTS.length}`:ispdf?'Local textbook PDF':'Study notes'}</small></div><div class="top-end"><button type="button" class="iconbtn" data-open="contents" aria-label="Open contents and topic index" title="Contents">${icon('list')}</button><button type="button" class="iconbtn" data-open="search" aria-label="Search chapters and topics" title="Search">${icon('search')}</button>${isreader?`<button type="button" class="iconbtn savebtn" id="bookmarkBtn" aria-pressed="${bookmarksActive}" aria-label="${bookmarksActive?'Remove bookmark':'Bookmark this guide'}" title="Bookmark">${icon('star')}</button>`:''}<button type="button" class="iconbtn" data-open="notes" aria-label="Open notes and highlights" title="Notes">${icon('note')}</button><button type="button" class="iconbtn" id="settingsBtn" aria-label="Reading appearance" title="Appearance">${icon('settings')}</button><button type="button" class="iconbtn" id="pdfBtn" aria-label="Open local textbook PDF" title="Textbook PDF">${icon('pdf')}</button></div>`;}
function updateBar(){ $('#topbar').innerHTML=navbar();applySidebarState();}
function renderLibrary(){currentPart=null;document.body.dataset.screen='library';$('#footerbar').hidden=true;$('#studySidebar').hidden=true;applySidebarState();document.title='Biological Psychology · Bookshelf | PsychDesk';let overall=state.done.length;let complete=CHAPTERS.filter(c=>chapterProgress(c).pct===100).length;const resume=PARTS.find(p=>p.id===state.resume?.partId)||PARTS[0];$('#viewport').innerHTML=`<div class="library"><div class="kicker">Psychology / Reading room</div><div class="library-heading"><h1>Your bookshelf<span style="color:var(--accent)">.</span></h1><p>Read chapters at your own pace, pick up where you left off, and keep highlights and notes in one place.</p></div><section class="featured" aria-labelledby="featuredTitle"><div class="book-cover" aria-label="Biological Psychology, 14th Edition, James W. Kalat"><span class="cover-edition">CENGAGE · 14TH EDITION</span><div class="cover-title">Biological<br/>Psychology</div><img class="cover-art" src="${COVER_ART}" alt="Colorful neural artwork from the textbook cover"/><div class="cover-foot">JAMES W. KALAT</div></div><div class="feature-copy"><div class="kicker">Featured textbook · personal edition</div><h2 id="featuredTitle">Biological Psychology</h2><p class="author">James W. Kalat</p><p class="description">Classroom-style explanations and source-based key points are available for every indexed topic across all 14 chapters. Search the full topic index. Bookmarks, highlights, notes, and achievements remain in this browser.</p><div class="chips"><span class="chip">14th edition · 2024</span><span class="chip">${CHAPTERS.length} chapters</span><span class="chip">${BOOK.coverage.indexedChapterHeadings} classroom topics</span></div><div class="big-progress" role="progressbar" aria-label="Reading progress" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="42" aria-valuenow="${overall}"><span style="width:${Math.round(100*overall/PARTS.length)}%"></span></div><div class="progress-caption"><span>${overall} of ${PARTS.length} study modules completed</span><span>${PARTS.length-overall} remaining</span></div><div class="feature-actions"><button type="button" class="primary" id="continueBtn">${overall?'Continue studying':'Start studying'}</button><button type="button" class="secondary" data-open="contents">Explore contents</button><button type="button" class="secondary" id="chooseBookPdf">Choose textbook PDF</button></div><p class="note-hint" style="margin-top:12px">Resume: ${escapeHTML(resume.title)} · ${fmtPage(resume.printedStart,resume.printedEnd)}</p></div></section><div class="stats-strip"><div class="stat"><strong>${complete} / ${CHAPTERS.length}</strong><span>Chapters completed</span></div><div class="stat"><strong>${overall} / ${PARTS.length}</strong><span>Study modules completed</span></div><div class="stat"><strong>${BOOK.coverage.indexedTopicHeadings}</strong><span>Verified index headings</span></div></div><div class="section-heading"><h2>Chapter shelves</h2><span>Read by module · classroom notes across all 14 chapters</span></div><div class="chapter-grid">${CHAPTERS.map(c=>{const v=chapterProgress(c);return `<article class="chapter-card" data-chapter="${c.number}"><div class="card-top"><span class="chap-number">${String(c.number).padStart(2,'0')}</span><span class="card-state">${v.read===v.total?'✓ Complete':v.read?`${v.read}/${v.total} read`:'Not started'}</span></div><h3>${escapeHTML(c.title)}</h3><span class="classroom-card-chip">CLASSROOM NOTES</span><div class="card-progress" role="progressbar" aria-label="Chapter ${c.number} completed modules" aria-valuenow="${v.read}" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="${v.total}"><span style="width:${v.pct}%"></span></div><div class="card-bottom"><span>${fmtPage(c.printedStart,c.printedEnd)}</span><button type="button" data-read="${c.modules.find(m=>!isDone(m.readingPartId))?.readingPartId||c.modules[0].readingPartId}">Read chapter →</button></div><details><summary>${v.total} study parts</summary>${c.modules.map(m=>`<button type="button" class="subpart-link" data-read="${m.readingPartId}"><small>${isDone(m.readingPartId)?'✓':m.number}</small><span>${escapeHTML(m.title)}</span></button>`).join('')}</details></article>`}).join('')}</div><div class="section-heading"><h2>Appendices & references</h2><span>Index-only reference sections</span></div><div class="backmatter">${BOOK.appendices.map(a=>`<article class="backcard"><div class="kicker">${escapeHTML(a.label)}</div><h3>${escapeHTML(a.title)}</h3><p>${fmtPage(a.printedStart,a.printedEnd)} · ${a.headings.length} indexed headings</p><button type="button" data-pdf="${a.pdfStart}">Open source in PDF →</button></article>`).join('')}</div><p class="library-footnote">Source-based detailed notes are organized by the textbook’s verified topic headings. This standalone offline copy saves progress locally, not to Google or Firestore. To view the source pages, choose the matching textbook PDF for this session. ${BOOK.backMatter.map(x=>escapeHTML(x.title)).join(' · ')} are also indexed in the contents.</p></div>`;updateBar();window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'instant'});$('#continueBtn').onclick=()=>readPart(resume.id,true);$('#chooseBookPdf').onclick=choosePdf;}
function partHighlights(p,idx,fullText){const list=state.highlights.filter(h=>h.partId===p.id&&h.paragraphIndex===idx).map(h=>({start:Math.max(0,+h.start||0),end:Math.min(fullText.length,+h.end||0)})).filter(x=>x.end>x.start).sort((a,b)=>a.start-b.start);let intervals=[];for(const h of list){let last=intervals[intervals.length-1];if(last&&h.start<=last.end)last.end=Math.max(last.end,h.end);else intervals.push({...h})}let out='',cursor=0;for(const z of intervals){out+=escapeHTML(fullText.slice(cursor,z.start));out+='<mark>'+escapeHTML(fullText.slice(z.start,z.end))+'</mark>';cursor=z.end}return out+escapeHTML(fullText.slice(cursor))}
function renderReader(part,restorePosition=true){
 currentPart=part;document.body.dataset.screen='reader';document.title=part.title+' · Biological Psychology Notes | PsychDesk';
 const chapter=CHAPTERS.find(c=>c.id===part.chapterId),mod=chapter.modules.find(m=>m.id===part.moduleId);
 const idx=PARTS.findIndex(p=>p.id===part.id),isread=isDone(part.id);
 const savedpos=state.positions[part.id]??(state.resume.partId===part.id?state.resume.percent:0)??0;
 const contentHTML=part.content.map((n,i)=>n.type==='heading'
   ? `<h2 id="${escapeHTML(n.id||('section-'+i))}" class="study-section-title">${escapeHTML(n.text)}</h2>`
   : `<p data-para="${i}">${partHighlights(part,i,n.text)}</p>`).join('');
 const overview=part.content.slice(0,2).map((n,i)=>`<p data-para="${i}">${partHighlights(part,i,n.text)}</p>`).join('');
 const mainNotes=part.content.slice(2,part.deepContentStart||part.content.length).map((n,j)=>{const i=j+2;return n.type==='heading'
   ? `<h2 id="${escapeHTML(n.id||('section-'+i))}" class="study-section-title">${escapeHTML(n.text)}</h2>`
   : `<p data-para="${i}">${partHighlights(part,i,n.text)}</p>`}).join('');
  const detailedTopics=(part.topicNotes||part.deepTopics||[]), detailedCount=detailedTopics.filter(t=>t.facts.length).length;
  const factsCount=detailedTopics.reduce((s,t)=>s+t.facts.length,0);
  const deepHTML=`<section class="source-deep" aria-labelledby="deepTitle"><span class="study-eyebrow">Full source-backed outline</span><h2 id="deepTitle">Detailed topic notes</h2><p class="deep-summary">Follow the textbook's actual headings and subheadings. Each topic includes source-derived study points, relevant definitions and a link to the printed page. These are selected and condensed textbook statements, alongside the original companion explanations above; figures and some studies still require the source.</p><div class="deep-counts"><span>${detailedCount} explained headings</span><span>${factsCount} sourced study points</span><span>${part.keyVocabulary?.length||0} related defined terms</span></div><div class="deep-controls" role="group" aria-label="Expand or collapse topic notes"><button type="button" id="expandTopicNotes">Expand all topics</button><button type="button" id="collapseTopicNotes">Collapse all topics</button></div>
  ${detailedTopics.map((t,i)=>`<details class="deep-topic" id="deep-topic-${t.id}" data-depth="${t.level}" open><summary><span class="topic-number">${String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')}</span><span class="topic-label">${escapeHTML(t.title)}</span><span class="topic-source">p. ${t.printedPage}</span></summary><div class="deep-topic-body"><div class="topic-lesson-subtitle">${t.parentId?'Subtopic study points':'Topic explanation'} · ${t.facts.length} points</div>${t.sourceStatus==='limited_intro_paragraph'?'<p class="topic-scope">This is a short introductory heading; the detailed discussion continues in its child topics.</p>':t.sourceStatus==='parent_overview_from_verified_subsections'?'<p class="topic-scope">This topic includes a short overview drawn from its verified textbook subheadings.</p>':''}${t.facts.length?t.facts.map(f=>`<p class="source-fact" data-para="${f.paragraphIndex}">${partHighlights(part,f.paragraphIndex,f.text)} <button type="button" class="fact-link" data-pdf="${f.pdfPage}" aria-label="Open textbook PDF at printed page ${f.printedPage}">p. ${f.printedPage} ↗</button></p>`).join(''):`<p class="no-text">This heading has a verified textbook page but reliable explanation text could not be separated. <button class="fact-link" type="button" data-pdf="${t.pdfPage}">Open p. ${t.printedPage} ↗</button></p>`}${t.glossary?.length?`<details class="topic-vocabulary"><summary>Key terms from the textbook · ${t.glossary.length} definitions</summary><dl>${t.glossary.map(g=>`<div><dt>${escapeHTML(g.term)}</dt><dd>${escapeHTML(g.definition)}</dd></div>`).join('')}</dl></details>`:''}<div class="topic-nav"><button type="button" data-pdf="${t.pdfPage}" aria-label="Open exact source for ${escapeHTML(t.title)}">See source, printed p. ${t.printedPage}</button></div></div></details>`).join('')}
  </section>`;
  const isClassroom=!!part.classroomNotes?.length;
 const classroomHTML=isClassroom?`<section class="classroom-module" aria-label="Classroom notes"><details class="classroom-map"><summary><span>In this module</span><span>${part.classroomNotes.length} topics</span></summary><ol>${part.classroomNotes.map(t=>`<li><a href="#classroom-topic-${t.headingId}">${escapeHTML(t.title)}</a></li>`).join('')}</ol></details>
 <div class="classroom-toolbar"><div><button id="expandClassroom" type="button">Expand all</button><button id="collapseClassroom" type="button">Collapse all</button></div></div>
 ${part.classroomNotes.map((t,i)=>`<details class="classroom-topic" id="classroom-topic-${t.headingId}" data-depth="${t.level}" open><summary><span class="classroom-counter">${String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')}</span><span>${escapeHTML(t.title)}</span><span class="classroom-page">p. ${t.printedPage}</span></summary><div class="classroom-topic-body">${t.editorialLesson?`<p class="classroom-editorial" data-para="${t.editorialLesson.paragraphIndex}">${partHighlights(part,t.editorialLesson.paragraphIndex,t.editorialLesson.text)}</p>`:''}<p data-para="${t.paragraphIndex}">${partHighlights(part,t.paragraphIndex,t.explanation)}</p>${t.followUpExplanation?`<p class="classroom-followup" data-para="${t.followUpParagraphIndex}">${partHighlights(part,t.followUpParagraphIndex,t.followUpExplanation)}</p>`:''}${t.editorialLesson?.guidedExplanation?`<details class="masterclass-walkthrough editorial-guided" open><summary>Step-by-step understanding</summary><div class="masterclass-body"><p class="masterclass-overview" data-para="${t.editorialLesson.guidedExplanation.overviewIndex}">${partHighlights(part,t.editorialLesson.guidedExplanation.overviewIndex,t.editorialLesson.guidedExplanation.overview)}</p><ol class="masterclass-process">${t.editorialLesson.guidedExplanation.steps.map(z=>`<li><h4>${escapeHTML(z.label)}</h4><p data-para="${z.paragraphIndex}">${partHighlights(part,z.paragraphIndex,z.explanation)}</p></li>`).join('')}</ol><div class="masterclass-origin"><button type="button" data-pdf="${t.editorialLesson.guidedExplanation.sourcePdfPage}">Textbook p. ${t.editorialLesson.guidedExplanation.sourcePrintedPage} ↗</button></div></div></details>`:''}<div class="topic-study-grid ${((part.classroomFigures||[]).some(f=>f.headingId===t.headingId))?'has-figure':''}"><div class="topic-study-points"><div class="classroom-label">Key points</div><ul class="classroom-bullets">${t.bullets.map(b=>`<li><strong>${escapeHTML(b.label)}</strong><p data-para="${b.paragraphIndex}">${partHighlights(part,b.paragraphIndex,b.explanation)}</p></li>`).join('')}</ul></div><div class="topic-study-figures">${(part.classroomFigures||[]).filter(f=>f.headingId===t.headingId).map(f=>`<figure class="book-figure"><button type="button" class="figure-view" data-figure="${f.id}" aria-label="Enlarge textbook figure ${escapeHTML(f.number)}: ${escapeHTML(f.title)}"><img src="${f.image}" alt="${escapeHTML(f.alt)}" width="${f.width}" height="${f.height}" loading="lazy" decoding="async"></button><figcaption><span><b>Figure ${escapeHTML(f.number)}</b> · ${escapeHTML(f.title)}</span><span><button type="button" data-pdf="${f.pdfPage}" aria-label="Open source PDF for figure ${escapeHTML(f.number)}">Textbook p. ${f.printedPage}</button><span class="figure-enlarge">Click to enlarge</span></span></figcaption></figure>`).join('')}</div></div>${t.masterclass?`<details class="masterclass-walkthrough" open><summary>In-depth explanation · step by step</summary><div class="masterclass-body"><p class="masterclass-overview" data-para="${t.masterclass.explanationIndex}">${partHighlights(part,t.masterclass.explanationIndex,t.masterclass.explanation)}</p><ol class="masterclass-process">${t.masterclass.points.map((x,i)=>`<li><h4>${escapeHTML(x.label)}</h4><p data-para="${x.paragraphIndex}">${partHighlights(part,x.paragraphIndex,x.explanation)}</p></li>`).join('')}</ol><div class="masterclass-takeaway"><strong>Important distinction</strong><p data-para="${t.masterclass.cautionIndex}">${partHighlights(part,t.masterclass.cautionIndex,t.masterclass.caution)}</p></div><div class="masterclass-question"><strong>Check your understanding</strong><p data-para="${t.masterclass.questionIndex}">${partHighlights(part,t.masterclass.questionIndex,t.masterclass.checkQuestion)}</p></div><div class="masterclass-origin">Based on the textbook section · <button type="button" data-pdf="${t.masterclass.sourcePdfPage}">printed p. ${t.masterclass.sourcePrintedPage} ↗</button></div></div></details>`:''}${(part.deeperStudy||[]).filter(d=>d.headingId===t.headingId&&d.additionalStudyPoints?.length).map(d=>`<details class="deeper-textbook-study"><summary><span>Explore more textbook detail</span><small>${d.additionalStudyPoints.length} extra points · source-linked</small></summary><div class="deeper-textbook-list">${d.additionalStudyPoints.map(x=>`<div class="deeper-study-point"><p data-para="${x.paragraphIndex}">${partHighlights(part,x.paragraphIndex,x.text)}</p><button type="button" data-pdf="${x.pdfPage}" aria-label="Open textbook page ${x.printedPage}">p. ${x.printedPage} ↗</button></div>`).join('')}</div></details>`).join('')}<button class="classroom-source" type="button" data-pdf="${t.pdfPage}" aria-label="Open textbook page ${t.printedPage} for ${escapeHTML(t.title)}">Check textbook · p. ${t.printedPage}</button></div></details>`).join('')}
 ${part.classroomResearch?`<aside class="classroom-research"><span class="classroom-label">Essential research example</span><h3>${escapeHTML(part.classroomResearch.title)}</h3><p>${escapeHTML(part.classroomResearch.body)}</p><button class="classroom-source" type="button" data-pdf="${part.classroomResearch.pdfPages[0]}">View source · p. ${part.classroomResearch.printedPages.join('–')}</button></aside>`:''}
 </section>`:'';
 const glossaryHTML=part.keyVocabulary?.length?`<details class="glossary-deep"><summary>Definitions from the original textbook glossary <span>${part.keyVocabulary.length} terms</span></summary><div class="glossary-entries">${part.keyVocabulary.map(g=>`<div><strong>${escapeHTML(g.term)}</strong><p>${escapeHTML(g.definition)}</p><button type="button" data-pdf="${g.sourcePdfPage}" aria-label="Open glossary on printed page ${g.sourcePrintedPage}">p. ${g.sourcePrintedPage}</button></div>`).join('')}</div></details>`:'';
  const objectivesHTML=part.learningObjectives?.length?`<details class="module-objectives"><summary>What you should understand <small>${part.learningObjectives.length} textbook learning objectives</small></summary><ol>${part.learningObjectives.map(g=>`<li>${escapeHTML(g.text)}</li>`).join('')}</ol></details>`:'';
  const recapHTML=part.chapterSummary?.length?`<details class="module-recap"><summary>End-of-module source recap <small>${part.chapterSummary.length} review points</small></summary><ol>${part.chapterSummary.map(g=>`<li>${escapeHTML(g.text)}</li>`).join('')}</ol></details>`:'';
 const checks=(part.selfCheck||[]).map((q,i)=>`<details class="self-check-item"><summary><span class="review-number">${String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')}</span> ${escapeHTML(q.question)}</summary><p>${escapeHTML(q.answer)}</p></details>`).join('');
 const moduleDiagramsHTML=(part.classroomFigures?.length?`<details class="module-diagrams"><summary><span>Module diagrams</span><small>${part.classroomFigures.length} textbook figure${part.classroomFigures.length===1?'':'s'}</small></summary><div class="module-figure-grid">${part.classroomFigures.map(f=>`<figure class="book-figure"><button type="button" class="figure-view" data-figure="${f.id}" aria-label="Enlarge textbook figure ${escapeHTML(f.number)}: ${escapeHTML(f.title)}"><img src="${f.image}" alt="${escapeHTML(f.alt)}" width="${f.width}" height="${f.height}" loading="lazy" decoding="async"></button><figcaption><span><b>Figure ${escapeHTML(f.number)}</b> · ${escapeHTML(f.title)}</span><span><button type="button" data-pdf="${f.pdfPage}">Textbook p. ${f.printedPage}</button></span></figcaption></figure>`).join('')}</div></details>`:'');
 $('#viewport').innerHTML=`<article class="reader-shell">
  <div class="reader-meta-row"><span class="eyebrow">Chapter ${String(chapter.number).padStart(2,'0')} · Module ${escapeHTML(part.moduleNumber)}</span><span class="source-pill">${fmtPage(part.printedStart,part.printedEnd)} · ${part.classroomNotes?.length?'Classroom notes':'Study notes'}</span></div>
  <h1 class="reader-title">${escapeHTML(part.title)}</h1><p class="reader-subtitle">${escapeHTML(chapter.title)}</p>
  <div class="reader-copy" id="guide-copy">
   <div class="reader-opening">${overview}</div>
   ${moduleDiagramsHTML}
   ${isClassroom?classroomHTML:`<div class="study-note-header"><span class="study-eyebrow">Essential study notes</span><span>${part.studySections?.length||0} explanations · ${fmtPage(part.printedStart,part.printedEnd)}</span></div>${mainNotes}`}
   ${isClassroom?`<details class="classroom-source-extra"><summary>Source notes <small>${factsCount} points</small></summary>${mainNotes}${deepHTML}</details>`:deepHTML}
   ${objectivesHTML}
   ${glossaryHTML}
    ${recapHTML}
  </div>
  <section class="self-check" aria-labelledby="selfCheckTitle"><div class="study-eyebrow">Active recall</div><h2 id="selfCheckTitle">Check your understanding</h2><p>Try answering from memory, then open an explanation. These are companion-written revision prompts, not copied textbook exercises.</p>${checks}</section>
  <div class="reading-context"><p>Based on Kalat, <em>Biological Psychology</em>, 14th edition. The figures and page references link to your textbook.</p><button type="button" class="secondary tiny" data-pdf="${part.pdfStart}">Open source ${fmtPage(part.printedStart,part.printedEnd)}</button></div>
  <details class="reference-index"><summary><span>Compact textbook heading index</span><small>${mod.headings.length} exact headings with printed-page links</small></summary>
   <section class="topic-section" aria-labelledby="topicTitle"><h2 id="topicTitle">Source headings</h2><p>Exact source headings and pages, alongside the detailed notes above. Use the topic list for fast reference.</p>
    <ul class="topic-list">${mod.headings.map(h=>`<li data-depth="${Math.min(3,h.level)}" id="topic-${h.id}"><div class="topic-row"><span class="topic-text">${escapeHTML(h.title)}</span><button type="button" class="page-link" data-pdf="${h.pdfPage}" aria-label="Open ${escapeHTML(h.title)} at printed page ${h.printedPage}">p. ${h.printedPage} ↗</button></div></li>`).join('')}</ul></section></details>
  <div class="reader-outro"><p>Study achievement · ${isread?'Marked as studied':'Not completed yet'}<br/>${fmtPage(part.printedStart,part.printedEnd)} · PDF viewer ${part.pdfStart}–${part.pdfEnd}</p>
   <div class="row-actions"><button type="button" id="markReadBtn" class="secondary read-finish" aria-pressed="${isread}">${isread?'✓ Marked studied':'✓ Mark as studied'}</button><button type="button" class="primary" id="nextPartBtn" ${idx===PARTS.length-1?'disabled':''}>${idx===PARTS.length-1?'End of book':'Next notes →'}</button></div></div>
 </article>`;
 $('#footerbar').hidden=false;renderFooter(part);updateBar();renderStudySidebar(part);applySidebarState();
 $('#markReadBtn').onclick=()=>toggleRead(part.id);$('#nextPartBtn').onclick=()=>{if(idx+1<PARTS.length)readPart(PARTS[idx+1].id)};
 $('#expandTopicNotes')?.addEventListener('click',()=>$$('.deep-topic').forEach(t=>t.open=true));$('#collapseTopicNotes')?.addEventListener('click',()=>$$('.deep-topic').forEach(t=>t.open=false));
 $('#expandClassroom')?.addEventListener('click',()=>$$('.classroom-topic').forEach(t=>t.open=true));$('#collapseClassroom')?.addEventListener('click',()=>$$('.classroom-topic').forEach(t=>t.open=false));
 requestAnimationFrame(()=>{if(restorePosition){let limit=Math.max(0,document.documentElement.scrollHeight-window.innerHeight),pct=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,savedpos));window.scrollTo({top:limit*pct/100,behavior:'instant'})}else window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'instant'});updateScrollProgress()});
}
function renderFooter(part){const idx=PARTS.findIndex(p=>p.id===part.id);$('#footerbar').innerHTML=`<div class="progress-track" aria-hidden="true"><span id="scrollBar"></span></div><div class="position-info" id="locationLabel">Notes ${idx+1} / ${PARTS.length} · ${fmtPage(part.printedStart,part.printedEnd)}</div><div class="foot-controls"><button type="button" id="prevBtn" ${idx===0?'disabled':''} aria-label="Previous reading part">← <span class="footer-word">Previous</span></button><button type="button" id="goBtn">Go to part</button><button type="button" id="nextBtn" ${idx===PARTS.length-1?'disabled':''} aria-label="Next reading part"><span class="footer-word">Next</span><span class="footer-short">→</span><span class="footer-word"> →</span></button></div>`;$('#prevBtn').onclick=()=>{if(idx>0)readPart(PARTS[idx-1].id)};$('#nextBtn').onclick=()=>{if(idx+1<PARTS.length)readPart(PARTS[idx+1].id)};$('#goBtn').onclick=openJump;}
function toggleRead(id){state.done=isDone(id)?state.done.filter(x=>x!==id):[...state.done,id];save();if(currentPart?.id===id){const pos=state.positions[id]??0;renderReader(currentPart);requestAnimationFrame(()=>{window.scrollTo({top:Math.max(0,document.documentElement.scrollHeight-innerHeight)*pos/100,behavior:'instant'})})}toast(isDone(id)?'Reading part completed':'Marked as not read')}
function toggleBookmark(){if(!currentPart)return;const id=currentPart.id;state.bookmarks=state.bookmarks.includes(id)?state.bookmarks.filter(x=>x!==id):[...state.bookmarks,id];save();updateBar();toast(state.bookmarks.includes(id)?'Bookmark saved':'Bookmark removed')}
function updateScrollProgress(){if(!currentPart||document.body.dataset.screen!=='reader')return;const max=Math.max(0,document.documentElement.scrollHeight-window.innerHeight);const pct=max?Math.round(Math.min(100,Math.max(0,scrollY/max*100))):100;if($('#scrollBar'))$('#scrollBar').style.width=pct+'%';if($('#locationLabel'))$('#locationLabel').textContent=`Notes ${PARTS.findIndex(p=>p.id===currentPart.id)+1}/${PARTS.length} · ${pct}% through part`;state.positions[currentPart.id]=pct;state.resume={partId:currentPart.id,percent:pct};save()}
let scrollPending=false;window.addEventListener('scroll',()=>{if(scrollPending)return;scrollPending=true;requestAnimationFrame(()=>{scrollPending=false;updateScrollProgress()})},{passive:true});
function renderPdf(n){currentPart=null;document.body.dataset.screen='pdf';$('#footerbar').hidden=true;$('#studySidebar').hidden=true;applySidebarState();currentPdfPage=Math.max(1,Math.min(BOOK.pageMapping.viewerPages,+n||21));document.title='Textbook PDF · Biological Psychology | PsychDesk';$('#viewport').innerHTML=`<section class="pdf-shell"><div class="pdf-header"><div><div class="kicker">Local source / PDF page ${currentPdfPage}</div><h1>Textbook source</h1><p>Printed p. ${currentPdfPage>19?currentPdfPage-18:'front matter'} · PDF viewer page ${currentPdfPage} · selected PDF is not uploaded anywhere</p></div><div class="pdf-actions"><button class="secondary" type="button" id="replacePdf">${pdfUrl?'Change PDF':'Choose PDF'}</button>${pdfUrl?'<button class="secondary" type="button" id="externalPdf">Open viewer in new tab ↗</button>':''}<button class="secondary" type="button" id="pdfReturn">Back to last notes</button></div></div>${pdfUrl?`<iframe class="pdf-frame" id="pdfFrame" title="Local Biological Psychology textbook PDF" src="${escapeHTML(pdfUrl)}#page=${currentPdfPage}&zoom=page-width"></iframe>`:`<div class="pdf-empty"><div class="kicker">Ready when you are</div><h2>Connect your local textbook</h2><p>Choose the supplied <em>Biological Psychology, 14th Edition</em> PDF from your device. This local file stays in this browser session and source links open the verified PDF viewer page.</p><button type="button" class="primary" id="inlineChoosePdf">Choose local PDF</button><p class="note-hint">The browser cannot automatically retain this PDF selection after reopening a standalone file.</p></div>`}</section>`;updateBar();window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'instant'});$('#replacePdf').onclick=choosePdf;if($('#inlineChoosePdf'))$('#inlineChoosePdf').onclick=choosePdf;if($('#externalPdf'))$('#externalPdf').onclick=()=>window.open(pdfUrl+'#page='+currentPdfPage+'&zoom=page-width','_blank','noopener');$('#pdfReturn').onclick=()=>readPart(state.resume?.partId||PARTS[0].id,true)}
function choosePdf(){let f=$('#pdfInput');f.value='';f.click()}
$('#pdfInput').addEventListener('change',e=>{const f=e.target.files?.[0];if(!f)return;if(!(/\.pdf$/i.test(f.name)||f.type==='application/pdf')){toast('Please choose a PDF file.');return}if(pdfUrl)URL.revokeObjectURL(pdfUrl);pdfUrl=URL.createObjectURL(f);pdfFileName=f.name;toast('PDF connected for this session');if(document.body.dataset.screen==='pdf')renderPdf(currentPdfPage)});
function renderRoute(){const h=getHash();const parts=h.match(/^read=(.+)$/);const pdf=h.match(/^pdf=(\d+)$/);closeDrawer(true);closeSelection();applySettings();if(parts){let p=PARTS.find(p=>p.id===parts[1])||PARTS[0];renderReader(p,true)}else if(pdf){renderPdf(Number(pdf[1]))}else{renderLibrary()}}
window.addEventListener('hashchange',renderRoute);
function showDrawer(type){closeDrawer(true);drawerType=type;drawerFocusRestore=document.activeElement;const target=$('#drawer');target.classList.toggle('right',type==='notes');target.innerHTML=`<div class="drawer-head"><h2 id="drawerTitle">${type==='contents'?'Contents':type==='search'?'Search the book':'Notes & highlights'}</h2><button class="iconbtn" type="button" id="drawerClose" aria-label="Close panel">${icon('close')}</button></div><div class="drawer-body" id="drawerBody"></div>`;target.setAttribute('aria-labelledby','drawerTitle');target.hidden=false;$('#scrim').hidden=false;$('#drawerClose').onclick=()=>closeDrawer();if(type==='contents')renderContentsDrawer();else if(type==='search')renderSearchDrawer();else renderNotesDrawer();setTimeout(()=>{const initial=type==='search'?$('#searchInput'):$('#drawerClose');initial?.focus()},0)}
function closeDrawer(silent=false){const wasOpen=!!drawerType;drawerType=null;$('#drawer').hidden=true;$('#scrim').hidden=true;if(wasOpen&&!silent&&drawerFocusRestore?.isConnected)drawerFocusRestore.focus();}
$('#scrim').addEventListener('click',()=>closeDrawer());
function renderContentsDrawer(){const el=$('#drawerBody');el.innerHTML=`<p class="drawer-hint">${CHAPTERS.length} chapters · ${PARTS.length} full study modules · ${BOOK.coverage.indexedTopicHeadings} indexed textbook headings. Source links work after choosing a local PDF.</p>${CHAPTERS.map(c=>`<details class="drawer-chapter" ${currentPart?.chapterId===c.id?'open':''}><summary><span class="num">${String(c.number).padStart(2,'0')}</span><span>${escapeHTML(c.title)}</span></summary>${c.modules.map(m=>`<div class="drawer-module"><button type="button" data-read="${m.readingPartId}" aria-current="${currentPart?.id===m.readingPartId?'true':'false'}"><b>${m.number}</b> · ${escapeHTML(m.title)} ${isDone(m.readingPartId)?'✓':''}</button><div class="drawer-sublist">${m.headings.map(h=>`<button type="button" data-topic="${h.id}" data-part="${m.readingPartId}">${escapeHTML(h.title)} <span class="sr-only">page ${h.printedPage}</span> <span style="color:var(--accent)">· ${h.printedPage}</span></button>`).join('')}</div></div>`).join('')}</details>`).join('')}<div class="note-group"><h3>Appendices & back matter</h3>${[...BOOK.appendices,...BOOK.backMatter].map(a=>`<div class="drawer-module"><button type="button" data-pdf="${a.pdfStart}">${escapeHTML(a.title)} · p. ${a.printedStart}</button>${(a.headings||[]).map(h=>`<div class="drawer-sublist"><button type="button" data-pdf="${h.pdfPage}">${escapeHTML(h.title)} · ${h.printedPage}</button></div>`).join('')}</div>`).join('')}</div>`}
function searchItems(term){let q=normSearch(term);if(!q)return[];const items=[];
 for(const c of CHAPTERS){
  if(normSearch(c.title).includes(q))items.push({kind:'Chapter',title:c.title,page:c.printedStart,partId:c.modules[0].readingPartId});
  for(const m of c.modules){
   const p=PARTS.find(p=>p.id===m.readingPartId);
   if(normSearch(m.title+' '+m.number).includes(q))items.push({kind:'Notes '+m.number,title:m.title,page:m.printedStart,partId:m.readingPartId});
   for(const s of (p?.studySections||[])) if(normSearch(s.title+' '+s.text).includes(q))items.push({kind:'Study explanation · '+m.number,title:s.title,page:s.sourcePrintedStart,partId:m.readingPartId,sectionId:s.id});
   for(const h of m.headings){const deep=(p?.deepTopics||[]).find(t=>t.id===h.id);if(normSearch(h.title+' '+(p?.classroomNotes||[]).filter(x=>x.headingId===h.id).map(x=>x.explanation+' '+(x.followUpExplanation||'')+' '+(x.editorialLesson?.text||'')+' '+(x.editorialLesson?.guidedExplanation?x.editorialLesson.guidedExplanation.overview+' '+x.editorialLesson.guidedExplanation.steps.map(z=>z.label+' '+z.explanation).join(' '):'')+' '+x.bullets.map(b=>b.label+' '+b.explanation).join(' ')+' '+(x.masterclass?x.masterclass.explanation+' '+x.masterclass.points.map(k=>k.label+' '+k.explanation).join(' ')+' '+x.masterclass.caution+' '+x.masterclass.checkQuestion:'')).join(' ')+' '+(deep?.facts||[]).map(f=>f.text).join(' ')+' '+(p?.deeperStudy||[]).filter(d=>d.headingId===h.id).flatMap(d=>d.additionalStudyPoints||[]).map(d=>d.text).join(' ')).includes(q))items.push({kind:(deep?.facts.length?'Detailed topic notes · ':'Textbook topic · ')+m.number,title:h.title,page:h.printedPage,partId:m.readingPartId,topicId:h.id});}
  }
 }
 for(const a of BOOK.appendices){if(normSearch(a.title).includes(q))items.push({kind:a.label,title:a.title,page:a.printedStart,pdfPage:a.pdfStart});for(const h of a.headings)if(normSearch(h.title).includes(q))items.push({kind:a.label,title:h.title,page:h.printedPage,pdfPage:h.pdfPage})}
 for(const a of BOOK.backMatter)if(normSearch(a.title).includes(q))items.push({kind:'Back matter',title:a.title,page:a.printedStart,pdfPage:a.pdfStart});
 return items;
}
function normSearch(s){return String(s).toLocaleLowerCase().normalize('NFKD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').replace(/[^a-z0-9 ]/g,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim()}
function renderSearchDrawer(){const el=$('#drawerBody');el.innerHTML=`<p class="drawer-hint">Search classroom explanations, in-depth walkthroughs, source notes, and topic titles.</p><label class="sr-only" for="searchInput">Search textbook contents</label><input class="searchbox" id="searchInput" type="search" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Try: amygdala, synapse, memory…" /><div id="results" class="results" aria-live="polite"></div>`;$('#searchInput').addEventListener('input',e=>{const term=e.target.value.trim();const found=searchItems(term).slice(0,120);$('#results').innerHTML=!term?`<p class="drawer-hint">Search ${BOOK.coverage.indexedTopicHeadings} indexed headings and ${BOOK.coverage.detailedFactPoints} detailed source points.</p>`:found.length?found.map(r=>`<button type="button" class="result" ${r.partId?`data-read="${r.partId}" ${r.topicId?`data-scrolltopic="${r.topicId}"`:''}${r.sectionId?`data-scrollsection="${r.sectionId}"`:''}`:`data-pdf="${r.pdfPage}"`}><small>${escapeHTML(r.kind)} · printed p. ${r.page}</small><strong>${escapeHTML(r.title)}</strong></button>`).join(''):`<p class="drawer-hint">No matching notes or headings. Try a shorter term.</p>`;});$('#searchInput').dispatchEvent(new Event('input'))}
function renderNotesDrawer(){const id=currentPart?.id||state.resume?.partId||PARTS[0].id;const part=PARTS.find(p=>p.id===id)||PARTS[0];const high=state.highlights.filter(h=>h.partId===id);$('#drawerBody').innerHTML=`<p class="drawer-hint">Private notes for <strong>${escapeHTML(part.title)}</strong>. Everything here is kept in this browser’s local storage.</p><label for="noteInput" style="display:block;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:9px">Notes for this note</label><textarea id="noteInput" class="notearea" placeholder="Write what you noticed or want to revisit...">${escapeHTML(state.notes[id]||'')}</textarea><p class="note-hint" id="noteSaved">Saved locally as you type</p><div class="note-group"><h3>Highlights (${high.length})</h3>${high.length?high.map(h=>`<div class="saved-quote"><span>“${escapeHTML(h.quote)}”</span><small>Note ${part.moduleNumber} · from this module</small><button type="button" data-remove-highlight="${escapeHTML(h.id)}">Remove highlight</button></div>`).join(''):'<p class="drawer-hint">Select a sentence in the reading note to highlight it.</p>'}</div><div class="note-group"><h3>Bookmarks (${state.bookmarks.length})</h3>${state.bookmarks.map(id=>{const p=PARTS.find(x=>x.id===id);return p?`<div class="drawer-module"><button type="button" data-read="${p.id}">☆ ${escapeHTML(p.moduleNumber)} · ${escapeHTML(p.title)}</button></div>`:''}).join('')||'<p class="drawer-hint">Bookmark a note with the star in the toolbar.</p>'}</div>`;$('#noteInput').addEventListener('input',e=>{state.notes[id]=e.target.value;save();$('#noteSaved').textContent='Saved locally ✓'});}
function classroomExtraParagraph(part,idx){for(const note of (part.classroomNotes||[])){if(note.editorialLesson?.paragraphIndex===idx)return note.editorialLesson.text;const g=note.editorialLesson?.guidedExplanation;if(g){if(g.overviewIndex===idx)return g.overview;for(const step of g.steps)if(step.paragraphIndex===idx)return step.explanation;}if(note.followUpParagraphIndex===idx)return note.followUpExplanation;for(const bullet of (note.bullets||[])){if(bullet.paragraphIndex===idx)return bullet.explanation}}for(const row of (part.deeperStudy||[])){for(const item of (row.additionalStudyPoints||[])){if(item.paragraphIndex===idx)return item.text}}for(const t of (part.classroomNotes||[])){let v=t.masterclass;if(!v)continue;if(v.explanationIndex===idx)return v.explanation;if(v.cautionIndex===idx)return v.caution;if(v.questionIndex===idx)return v.checkQuestion;for(const q of v.points)if(q.paragraphIndex===idx)return q.explanation}return undefined;}
function saveSelectionDraft(){if(!currentPart||document.body.dataset.screen!=='reader')return null;const selection=window.getSelection();if(!selection||selection.isCollapsed||!selection.toString().trim())return null;const range=selection.getRangeAt(0);const startEl=range.startContainer.nodeType===1?range.startContainer:range.startContainer.parentElement;const endEl=range.endContainer.nodeType===1?range.endContainer:range.endContainer.parentElement;const p=startEl?.closest?.('#guide-copy p[data-para]');const endp=endEl?.closest?.('#guide-copy p[data-para]');if(!p||p!==endp)return null;const before=document.createRange();before.selectNodeContents(p);before.setEnd(range.startContainer,range.startOffset);const start=before.toString().length,end=start+selection.toString().length,idx=Number(p.dataset.para);const source=currentPart.content[idx]?.text??(idx>=20000?currentPart.classroomParagraphs?.[idx-20000]:undefined)??classroomExtraParagraph(currentPart,idx);if(!source||start<0||end>source.length)return null;return {partId:currentPart.id,paragraphIndex:idx,start,end,quote:source.slice(start,end)};}
function closeSelection(){selectionDraft=null;$('#selectionMenu').hidden=true}

document.addEventListener('mouseup',()=>setTimeout(inspectSelection,0));document.addEventListener('keyup',e=>{if(e.key==='Shift'||e.key==='ArrowLeft'||e.key==='ArrowRight')setTimeout(inspectSelection,0)});

function jumpPreview(partId){const p=PARTS.find(p=>p.id===partId)||PARTS[0];const m=CHAPTERS.flatMap(c=>c.modules).find(m=>m.id===p.moduleId);$('#preview').innerHTML=`<div class="kicker">Chapter ${p.moduleNumber.split('.')[0]} · Module ${p.moduleNumber}</div><h3>${escapeHTML(p.title)}</h3><p>${fmtPage(p.printedStart,p.printedEnd)} · PDF pages ${p.pdfStart}–${p.pdfEnd} · ${m.headings.length} explained topic headings · ${p.coverageSummary?.factNotes||0} study points</p><ul>${m.headings.slice(0,5).map(h=>`<li>${escapeHTML(h.title)}</li>`).join('')}${m.headings.length>5?`<li>And ${m.headings.length-5} more headings…</li>`:''}</ul>`}

// Desktop-first chapter outline; no changes to book storage, resume IDs or annotation anchors.
function renderStudySidebar(part){
 const side=$('#studySidebar');
 const done=state.done.length;
 const pct=Math.round(100*done/PARTS.length);
 side.innerHTML=`<div class="study-side-head"><div class="study-side-label">CONTENTS</div><div style="display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center"><button type="button" class="study-side-search" data-open-side-search aria-label="Search the textbook">${icon('search')}</button><button type="button" class="study-side-search" data-toggle-study-sidebar aria-label="Collapse contents" title="Collapse contents">${icon('back')}</button></div></div>
 <div class="study-side-book"><strong>Biological Psychology</strong><span>James W. Kalat · 14th edition</span></div>
 <nav class="study-chapter-list" aria-label="Chapters and topics">${CHAPTERS.map(c=>`<details class="study-chapter" ${c.id===part.chapterId?'open':''}>
 <summary><span class="study-chapter-label">CHAPTER ${c.number}</span><span class="study-chapter-title">${escapeHTML(c.title)}</span></summary>
 <div class="study-module-list">${c.modules.map(m=>`<details class="study-module" ${m.id===part.moduleId?'open':''}>
 <summary><span class="study-module-no">${escapeHTML(m.number)}</span><span class="study-module-title">${escapeHTML(m.title)}</span>${isDone(m.readingPartId)?'<span class="study-check" aria-label="Studied">✓</span>':''}</summary>
 <button type="button" class="study-module-open" data-study-part="${m.readingPartId}">Read module overview</button>
 <div class="study-topic-list">${m.headings.map(h=>`<button type="button" class="study-topic ${m.id===part.moduleId?'study-topic-current-module':''}" data-study-part="${m.readingPartId}" data-study-topic="${h.id}" title="Go to ${escapeHTML(h.title)}"><span class="study-topic-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span><span>${escapeHTML(h.title)}</span></button>`).join('')}</div>
 </details>`).join('')}</div></details>`).join('')}</nav>
 <div class="study-side-footer"><div class="study-side-progress"><span>Study progress</span><strong>${done} / ${PARTS.length} modules</strong></div><div class="study-side-track"><span style="width:${pct}%"></span></div></div>`;
 side.hidden=false;
}
$('#studySidebar').addEventListener('click', e=>{
 const search=e.target.closest('[data-open-side-search]');if(search){showDrawer('search');return;}
 const collapse=e.target.closest('[data-toggle-study-sidebar]');if(collapse){state.sidebarCollapsed=true;save();applySidebarState();return;}
 const button=e.target.closest('[data-study-part]');if(!button)return;
 const id=button.dataset.studyPart;
 const topic=button.dataset.studyTopic;
 if(id!==currentPart?.id)readPart(id);
 if(topic){
  const jump=()=>{
   const el=document.getElementById('classroom-topic-'+topic)||document.getElementById('deep-topic-'+topic)||document.getElementById('topic-'+topic);
   if(el){if(el.tagName==='DETAILS')el.open=true;el.scrollIntoView({block:'start',behavior:'smooth'});
    const anchor=el.querySelector('summary')||el;anchor.setAttribute('tabindex',anchor.getAttribute('tabindex')||'-1');anchor.focus({preventScroll:true});}
  };
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(jump));
 }else if(id===currentPart?.id){window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'smooth'});}
});

applySettings();

if(!location.hash)history.replaceState(null,'','#library');renderRoute();
</script>
</body>
</html>
